  Keep track of # of page views.

    // Begin count once gdpr popup has been accepted.
    if("gdpr_accepted" in localStorage){

      // Get current page view count from storage, as int.
      var count = parseInt(localStorage["thinx_page_view_count"]);

      // Increment count or set to 1 if not set already.
      count = (count + 1) || 1;

      // Update storage to reflect new count.
      localStorage["thinx_page_view_count"] = count;

}

    'user_email': document.getElementsByName('customer[email]')[0].value
    });
    snaptr('track', 'SIGN_UP');
}

    function identify(){

      // Set createdAt prop if the customer does not have an account.
if("friendbuy" in window){

        window['friendbuy'].push(['track', 'customer',
          {
            id:    '',
            email: ''
          }
        ]);

      }
}

    function addToCart(variant, qty, product, brand){

      var price = variant.price;
      qty       = parseInt(qty);

      // Apply custom set price.
      if (qty == 3 || qty == 4) {
        price = price * .9;
      } else if (qty == 5 || qty == 6) {
        price = price * .85;
      } else if (qty >= 7) {
        price = price * .8;
      }

      price = price / 100;

      fbq('track', 'AddToCart', {
        value:          price,
        currency:       "USD",
        content_name:   variant.title,
        // content_ids: [variant.sku],
        content_type:   "product",
        brand:          brand,
        contents: [
          {
            quantity: qty,
            id:       variant.sku
          }
        ]
      });

      fbq('track', brand + 'AddToCart', {
        value:          price,
        currency:       "USD",
        content_name:   variant.title,
        // content_ids: [variant.sku],
        content_type:   "product",
        brand:          brand,
        contents: [
          {
            quantity: qty,
            id:       variant.sku
          }
        ]
      });

      pintrk('track', 'addtocart', {
        value:          price,
        order_quantity: qty,
        currency:       'USD',
        line_items: [
          {
            product_id: variant.sku,
            product_quantity: qty,
            product_price: price
          }
        ]
      });

      pintrk('track', brand + 'AddToCart', {
        value:          price,
        order_quantity: qty,
        currency:       'USD',
        line_items: [
          {
            product_id: variant.sku,
            product_quantity: qty,
            product_price: price
          }
        ]
      });

      DY.API('event', {
        name: 'Add to Cart',
        properties: {
          dyType: 'add-to-cart-v1',
          // Total value in actual payment currency, as float (dollars dot cents)
          value: price,
          currency: 'USD',
          // SKU exactly as in the product feed!
          productId: variant.sku.slice(0, -2),
          quantity: qty
        }
      });

      window.dotq = window.dotq || [];
      window.dotq.push(
      {
       'projectId': '10000',
       'properties': {
         'pixelId': '10032280',
         'qstrings': {
           'et': 'custom',
           'ea': 'AddToCart',
           'product_id': variant.sku,
         }
      } } );

      obApi('track', 'Add to Cart');

      // GTM.
      dataLayer.push({
        'event': 'addtocart',
        'u1': product.type,
        'u2': product.title,
        'u3': variant.sku,
        'u4': price
      });

    }

    function trackHowItWorksVideo(id){

      var player = new Vimeo.Player(id);
      var videoTitle, videoID;

      player.on('pause', function(data){

        player.getVideoTitle().then(function(title) {

          videoTitle = title;

          player.getVideoId().then(function(id) {

            videoID = id

            heap.track('How It Works Video Paused', {
              videoTitle:     videoTitle,
              videoID:        videoID,
              percentWatched: (data.percent * 100)
            });

            DY.API('event', {
              name: 'How It Works Video Paused',
              properties: {
                dyType:          'video-watch-v1',
                autoplay:        false,
                progress:        'VIDEO_PROGRESS',
                progressPercent: (data.percent * 100)
              }
            });

          });

        });

      });

    }

    function trackHompageVideo(){

      heap.track('Homepage Video Click', {
        videoTitle:   'A day in the life of a real menstruating human',
        vimeoVideoID: 'c9z2IxJV1lA'
      });

      DY.API('event', {
        name: 'Homepage Video Click',
        properties: {
          dyType:   'video-watch-v1',
          autoplay: false
        }
      });

    }

    function trackCXPages(){

      var search = location.search;

      // Search for ref=cx in href.
      if(search.indexOf("ref=cx") !== -1){

        // Track CX page view.
        heap.track("CX link view", {
          location: location.href
        });

        // Thinx size chart view.
        if(location.href.indexOf("thinx-size-chart") !== -1){

          // Track CX page view.
          heap.track("CX size chart view", {
            location: location.href
          });

        }

        // Thinx get $10 view.
        if(location.href.indexOf("thinx-get-10") !== -1){

          // Track CX page view.
          heap.track("CX get 10 view", {
            location: location.href
          });

        }

        // Thinx KYF view.
        if(location.href.indexOf("thinx-know-your-flow") !== -1){

          // Track CX page view.
          heap.track("CX KYF view", {
            location: location.href
          });

        }

        // Thinx giverise view.
        if(location.href.indexOf("thinx-giverise") !== -1){

          // Track CX page view.
          heap.track("CX giverise view", {
            location: location.href
          });

        }

        // Thinx store locator view.
        if(location.href.indexOf("thinx-retail-store-locator") !== -1){

          // Track CX page view.
          heap.track("CX store locator view", {
            location: location.href
          });

        }

        // Thinx homepage view.
        if(location.pathname === "/" || location.pathname === ""){

          // Track CX page view.
          heap.track("CX homepage view", {
            location: location.href
          });

        }

      }

    }

    function pageUnload(){
      heap.track('pageExited');
    }

    return {
      addToCart:                   addToCart,
      pageView:                    pageView,
      pinterestPageView:           pinterestPageView,
      productView:                 productView,
      collectionView:              collectionView,
      blogView:                    blogView,
      articleView:                 articleView,
      cartView:                    cartView,
      pinterestCartView:           pinterestCartView,
      accountView:                 accountView,
      pinterestAccountView:        pinterestAccountView,
      checkoutView:                checkoutView,
      pinterestCheckoutView:       pinterestCheckoutView,
      checkoutStarted:             checkoutStarted,
      pinterestCheckoutStarted:    pinterestCheckoutStarted,
      checkoutStepViewed:          checkoutStepViewed,
      pinterestCheckoutStepViewed: pinterestCheckoutStepViewed,
      identify:                    identify,
      trackHowItWorksVideo:        trackHowItWorksVideo,
      trackHompageVideo:           trackHompageVideo,
      trackCXPages:                trackCXPages,
      pageUnload:                  pageUnload
    };

  })();

$(document).one("cart.ready", function(e, cart){

      $(document).on("click", '.js-cart-preview-item-minus', function() {
        var product = $(this).data("wholeitem");
        CartState.simon_adjust_cart(product, product.id, "THINX", "decrement", 1, "cart-preview");
      });

        var product = $(this).data("wholeitem");

      });

  });

<script>
// hide friend buy link if the user is in the countries specified in hideFriendBuy
  $(document).ready(function(){
    var $getTenLink = $(".js-get-10-link");

    var hideFriendBuy = {
      'Ivory Coast': 'CI',
      'Togo':        'TG',
      'Ghana':       'GH',
      'Nigeria':     'NG',
      'China':       'CN',
      'Hong Kong':   'HK',
      'Philippines': 'PH',
      'Russia':      'RU'
    }

    if (Object.values(hideFriendBuy).includes(window.DY.geoCode)) {
      $getTenLink.hide()
    }
  });
</script>

<script>
(function(){

    "use-strict";

    var $survey          = $(".survey");
    var $close           = $(".survey__close");
    var visible          = "survey--visible";
    var $firstForm       = $(".new-visitor-survey__first-form");
    var $secondForm      = $(".new-visitor-survey__second-form");

    var close = function(){
      $survey.removeClass(visible);
    };

    var open = function(){
      $survey.addClass(visible);
    };

    var listeners = function(){

      // Close button ev.
      $close.on("click", function(){
        close();
      });

      // AJAX submit for main form.
      $('#new-visitor-form').on("submit", function(e) {

        e.preventDefault();

        var formEl       = $(this);
        var submitButton = $('button[type=submit]', formEl);
        var heardFrom    = [];

        // Loop thru all checkbox inputs.
        formEl.find("[type='checkbox']").each(function(index, checkbox){

          // Make sure it's checked and push value of checkbox to array.
          if( $(checkbox).is(":checked") ){
            heardFrom.push( $(checkbox).val() );
          }

        });

        // Set heard-from hidden input value to heardFrom array. This will be a comma separated list of the checkbox values.
        formEl.find("[name='heard-from']").val(heardFrom).trigger("change");

        $.ajax({
          type: 'POST',
          url: formEl.prop('action'),
          accept: {
            javascript: 'application/javascript'
          },
          data: formEl.serialize(),
          beforeSend: function() {
            submitButton.prop('disabled', 'disabled');
          }
        }).done(function(data) {

          // Set flag in storage.
          localStorage["new_visitor_survey_submitted"] = true;

          // Show secondary survey if social media checkbox is selected.
          if( $("#social-media").is(":checked") ){
            $firstForm.addClass("new-visitor-survey__first-form--hidden");
            $secondForm.addClass("new-visitor-survey__second-form--visible");
          } else {
            // Close form.
            close();
          }

        });

      });

      // AJAX submit for secondary form.
      $('#new-visitor-secondary-form').on("submit", function(e) {

        e.preventDefault();

        var formEl       = $(this);
        var submitButton = $('button[type=submit]', formEl);
        var heardFrom    = [];

        // Loop thru all checkbox inputs.
        formEl.find("[type='checkbox']").each(function(index, checkbox){

          // Make sure it's checked and push value of checkbox to array.
          if( $(checkbox).is(":checked") ){
            heardFrom.push( $(checkbox).val() );
          }

        });

        // Set heard-from hidden input value to heardFrom array. This will be a comma separated list of the checkbox values.
        formEl.find("[name='heard-from']").val(heardFrom).trigger("change");

        $.ajax({
          type: 'POST',
          url: formEl.prop('action'),
          accept: {
            javascript: 'application/javascript'
          },
          data: formEl.serialize(),
          beforeSend: function() {
            submitButton.prop('disabled', 'disabled');
          }
        }).done(function(data) {
          // Close form.
          close();
        });

      });

    };

    // Generates a random number that we assign to new users.
    var generateKey = function(){
      return Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.floor( Date.now() ));
    };

    var init = function(){

      // Show survey based on criteria below.
      if("new_visitor_survey_submitted" in localStorage === false && parseInt(localStorage["thinx_page_view_count"]) === 2 && "dy_show_survey" in localStorage){

        // Init ev listeners.
        listeners();

        // Open survey.
        open();

        // Set a key in storage to track the user to checkout and determine conversions.
        if("thinx_inc_user_key" in localStorage === false){
          localStorage["thinx_inc_user_key"] = generateKey();
          $('[name="thinx-inc-user-key"]').val(localStorage["thinx_inc_user_key"]);
        }

      }

    };

    $(document).ready(function(){
      init();
    });

  })();
</script>

</div><script>
(function(){

    var $popup      = $(".content-warning-popup");
    var visible     = "content-warning-popup--visible";
    var proceedHref = "";

    // Check for is_from_btwn key in localStorage. This is set in layout/btwn.liquid.
    var isFromBtwn = function(){
      return "is_from_btwn" in localStorage;
    };

    var close = function(){
      $popup.removeClass(visible);
    };

    var open = function(){
      $popup.addClass(visible);
    };

    var events = function(){

      // Close popup.
      $(".js-warning-close").on("click", function(){
        close();
      });

      // When proceed btn is clicked, sent user to originally clicked link and remember their choice.
      $(".js-warning-proceed").on("click", function(){
        localStorage["blog_warning_accepted"] = true;
        window.location = proceedHref;
      });

      // Checks if the user came from BTWN and they haven't already accepted the blog content warning.
      if( isFromBtwn() && "blog_warning_accepted" in localStorage === false){

        $("a").on("click", function(e){

          var href = $(this).attr('href');

          // If href links to a blog or blog related page.
          if(href.indexOf("periodical") !== -1 || href.indexOf("blogs") !== -1){
            e.preventDefault();
            proceedHref = href;
            open();
          }

        });

      }

    };

    $(document).ready(function(){
      events();
    });

  })();
</script>

<script>
(function () {

        "use-strict";

        var slicked = false;
        var $mobileCarousel = $('.js-mobile-home-carousel');
        var $carousel = $('.js-carousel');
        var $heroTextWrap = $(".index-hero__text-wrap");
        var $heroText = $('.index-hero__text');
        var instaData = [];

        var slickSettings = {
            arrows: true,
            prevArrow: '<i class="fa fa-angle-left  icon__previous"></i>',
            nextArrow: '<i class="fa fa-angle-right  icon__next"></i>',
            autoplay: true,
            autoplaySpeed: 6000,
            fade: true,
            dots: true,
            mobileFirst: true,
            pauseOnHover: true,
            pauseOnFocus: true,
            lazyLoad: 'ondemand'
        };

        /*
        Determines if we should slick the mobile carousel element.
        */
        var shouldSlick = function () {
            return matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches;
        };

        /*
        Sets the height of the hero text wrapper. We need an explicit height set because its children are absolute positioned.
        */
        var setTextWrapHeight = function (currentSlide) {

            var $currentHeroText = $heroText.filter("[data-slide='" + currentSlide + "']");

            var height = $currentHeroText.outerHeight(true);

            $heroTextWrap.css("height", height);

        };

        /*
        Slicks/unslicks the mobile carousel based on the shouldSlick fn.
        */
        var runSlickSetup = function () {

            if (shouldSlick()) {

                // It should be slicked and it's not already slicked. Slick!
                if (!slicked) {
                    $mobileCarousel.slick(slickSettings);
                    slicked = true;
                }

            } else {

                // Should not be slicked and is currently slicked. Unslick!
                if (slicked) {
                    $mobileCarousel.slick("unslick");
                    slicked = false;
                }

            }

        };

        $carousel.on('beforeChange', function (e, slick) {

            // Get current slide and video in current slide.
            var $current = slick.$slides.filter(".slick-active");
            var $video = $current.find("video");

            // Pause video in current slide as before we move to the next slide.
            if ($video.length) {
                $video[0].pause();
            }

        })
        .on("afterChange", function (e, slick, currentSlide) {

            // 1 based index.
            currentSlide = currentSlide + 1;

            // Get current slide and video in current slide.
            var $current = slick.$slides.filter(".slick-active");
            var $video = $current.find("video");

            // Get current hero text element based on slide index.
            var $currentHeroText = $heroText.filter("[data-slide='" + currentSlide + "']");

            // Hide other hero text.
            $heroText.removeClass("index-hero__text--visible");

            // Set hero text wrapper height.
            setTextWrapHeight(currentSlide);

            // Show current hero text.
            $currentHeroText.addClass("index-hero__text--visible");

            // If there's a video, play in current slide.
            if ($video.length) {
                $video[0].play();
            }

        })
        .on("init", function (e, slick) {

            var currentSlide1 = slick.currentSlide + 1;
            var $current = slick.$slides.filter(".slick-active");
            var $video = $current.find("video");

            // Set hero text wrapper height.
            $(window).on("resize", function () {
                setTextWrapHeight($carousel.slick("slickCurrentSlide") + 1);
            });

            // Set hero text wrapper height.
            setTextWrapHeight(currentSlide1);

            if ($video.length) {
                $video[0].play();
            }

        });

        parseIgData = function (arr) {
            const r = [];
            for (let item of arr) {
                const o = {
                    src: '',
                    likeCount: 0,
                    id: 0,
                };
                o.src = item.thumbnail_resources[3].src;
                o.likeCount += item.edge_media_preview_like.count;
                o.id += item.id;
                r.push(o);
            }
            updatePage(r);
            updateDeepLinks(r);
            return r;
        }

        var numberWithCommas = function (num) {
            return num.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ",");
        }

        updatePage = function (arr) {
            const $srcs      = $('.js-instagram-card-img');
            const $likes     = $('.js-instagram-card-likes');

            $srcs.each(function (index, value){
                $(this).attr('src', arr[index].src);
            });

            $likes.each(function (index, value) {
                $(this).text(numberWithCommas(arr[index].likeCount));
            })

        }

        updateDeepLinks = function (arr) {

            const $deepLinks = $('.js-instagram-deep-link');

            // deep links should only be available on mobile devices with Instagram app

            if (shouldSlick() && !localStorage["_dy_device"].includes("desktop")) {
                $deepLinks.each(function (index, value){
                    $(this).attr('href', `instagram://media?id=${arr[index].id}`);
                })
            } else {
                $deepLinks.each(function (index, value){
                    $(this).attr('href', 'https://www.instagram.com/shethinx');
                })
            }
        }

        const igRequest = function() {
            $.ajax({
                url: "https://www.instagram.com/shethinx/?__a=1",
                type: "GET",
                success: function(data) {
                    // remove video posts from the most recent 12 IG posts
                    var filterOutVideos = data.graphql.user.edge_owner_to_timeline_media.edges.filter(edge => edge.node.is_video === false)

                    var r = [
                        filterOutVideos[0].node,
                        filterOutVideos[1].node,
                        filterOutVideos[2].node
                    ]
                    instaData = r;
                    parseIgData(instaData);
                },
                error: function(error) {
                    console.log(`Error ${error}`)
                }
            })
        }

        $(document).ready(function () {

            igRequest();

            runSlickSetup();

            // Normal slick init for $carousel element.
            $carousel.slick(slickSettings);

        });

        $(window).on("resize", function () {
            runSlickSetup();
        });

    })();

    $(".js-homepage-video").on("click", function () {
        pixelTracking.trackHompageVideo();
    })
</script>
</main>

<script>
$(document).ready(function(){

    var $form = $('#email_signup');

  	$form.submit(function(event) {

			event.preventDefault();

			// Grab geo info from Dynamic Yield and toss it into an object for sendGrid
			var geoData  = DY.geoFields;
			var userInfo = {};

			var today  = new Date();
	    var dd     = today.getDate();
	    var mm     = today.getMonth()+1; //January is 0!
	    var yyyy   = today.getFullYear();

	    if(dd<10){
        dd='0'+dd;
	    }

	    if(mm<10){
        mm='0'+mm;
	    }

	    today = mm+'/'+dd+'/'+yyyy;

			userInfo.email          = $("#email").val();
			userInfo.signup         = $(".footer-email-location").val();
			userInfo.geocity        = geoData.city;
			userInfo.geostate       = geoData.state;
			userInfo.geocountry     = geoData.country;
			userInfo.dateaddedthinx = today;

			var urlEncodedData = $.param(userInfo);

			console.log(userInfo);

			$.ajax({
				url:      $form.attr("action"),
				method:   $form.attr("method"),
				data:     urlEncodedData,
				dataType: 'html',
				async:    true,
			}).done(function(response) {

				$('.success').show();
				$('.error').hide();

				$form[0].reset();

        // Make heap.identify call with just their email.
        heap.identify(userInfo.email, 'email');

				// Simon.
				sd.identify({
			    email: userInfo.email
				});

				sd.track('registration', {
	        email: userInfo.email,
	        properties: {
            account: 'email',
						thinx_marketing: "true"
	        }
        });

				fbq('track', 'Lead');

			}).fail(function(data) {
				$('.success').hide();
				$('.error').show();
			});

		});

	});
</script>

<script>
(function(){

    // Currency option element.
    var $currency      = $(".js-currency-select-option");
    var $summary       = $(".currency-select-dropdown__summary");
    var $dropdown      = $(".currency-select-dropdown");
    var dropdownActive = "currency-select-dropdown--active";

    /*
    Adds active class to given active currency.
    */
    var activateCurrency = function($activeCurrency){

      // Check for $activeCurrency param length.
      if($activeCurrency.length){

        var currencyCode = $activeCurrency.data("currency");

        // Change currency with GIP.
        GIP.selectCurrency(currencyCode);

        // Close dropdown.
        $dropdown.removeClass(dropdownActive);

        // Update summary text.
        $summary.find(".currency-select__option")
        .html( $activeCurrency.html() );

      }

    };

    /*
    Updates dropdown summary element to reflect user's currency.
    */
    var setDefaultCurrency = function(){

      // Make sure user currency is in Storage.
      if("GIP_USER_CURRENCY" in localStorage){

        // Get currency select element with matching user currency.
        var $activeCurrency = $currency.filter("[data-currency='" + localStorage.GIP_USER_CURRENCY + "']");

        // Update summary text.
        if($activeCurrency.length){
          $summary.find(".currency-select__option")
          .html( $activeCurrency.html() );
        }

      }

    };

    var events = function(){

      // Dropdown summary click.
      $summary.on("click", function(e){
        e.preventDefault();
        $dropdown.toggleClass(dropdownActive);
      });

      // Change currency on currency el click.
      $currency.on("click", function(e){

        e.preventDefault();

        // Set active currency.
        activateCurrency( $(this) );

      });

    };

    /*
    Change currency on init. GIP (gointerpay) remembers which currency was previously selected, so let's honor that by updating the order of currencies to reflect that.
    */
    var init = function(){

      // If GIP is not present for whatever reason, hide currency selector and exit.
      if("GIP" in window === false){
        $(".currency-select").remove();
        console.log("Removing currency selector, can't find GIP in window.");
        return false;
      }

      events();
      setDefaultCurrency();

    };

    init();

  })();
</script>

<script>
$(document).ready(function(){

		// Show on 3rd page view.
    if( parseInt(localStorage["thinx_page_view_count"]) === 3 ){

      var t = window.setTimeout(function() {
				clearTimeout(t);
        $('.js-alert--email-prompt').addClass('active');
      }, 4000);

    }

    var $form = $('#email_signup_popup');

		$form.submit(function(event) {

			event.preventDefault();

			// Grab geo info from Dynamic Yield and toss it into an object for sendGrid.
			var geoData  = DY.geoFields;
			var userInfo = {};

			var today  = new Date();
	    var dd     = today.getDate();
	    var mm     = today.getMonth()+1; //January is 0!
	    var yyyy   = today.getFullYear();

	    if(dd<10){
        dd='0'+dd;
	    }

	    if(mm<10){
        mm='0'+mm;
	    }

	    var today = mm+'/'+dd+'/'+yyyy;

			userInfo.email          = $("#email_popup").val();
			userInfo.signup         = $(".popup-email-location").val();
			userInfo.geocity        = geoData.city;
			userInfo.geostate       = geoData.state;
			userInfo.geocountry     = geoData.country;
			userInfo.dateaddedthinx = today;

			var urlEncodedData = $.param(userInfo);

			$.ajax({
				url:      $form.attr("action"),
				method:   $form.attr("method"),
				data:     urlEncodedData,
				dataType: 'html',
				async:    true,
			}).done(function(response) {

				$form[0].reset();

				$('.success_popup').show();
				$('.error_popup').hide();

        // Make heap.identify call with just their email.
        heap.identify(userInfo.email, 'email');

				//simon
				sd.identify({
			    email: userInfo.email
				});

				sd.track('registration', {
          email: userInfo.email,
          properties: {
            account: 'email',
						thinx_marketing: "true"
          }
        });

			}).fail(function(data) {
				$('.success_popup').hide();
				$('.error_popup').show();
			});

		});

	});
</script>

<script>
(function(){

    var $accept = $('.js-gdpr-accept');
    var $popup  = $(".alert__gdpr");

    // Array of countries we don't want to show the popup to. Spelling of keys (countries) here should match the spelling that DY.geoFields.country returns.
    var excludedCountries = ["United States", "Australia", "Canada"];

    var initViewCount = function(){

      // Set view count to 1 here since we dont show the gdpr popup.
      if( !("thinx_page_view_count" in localStorage) ){
        localStorage["thinx_page_view_count"] = 1;
      }

    };

    // Hide popup on click and remember.
    $accept.on("click", function(){
      $popup.removeClass("active");
      initViewCount();
      localStorage["gdpr_accepted"] = true;
    });

    $(window).on("load", function(){

      // Don't show popup if user is in an excluded country.
      if("geoFields" in DY && "country" in DY.geoFields){

        if(excludedCountries.indexOf(DY.geoFields.country) !== -1){
          initViewCount();
          localStorage["gdpr_accepted"] = true;
          return false;
        }

      }

      // Show popup if storage key not found.
      if( !("gdpr_accepted" in localStorage) ){
        $popup.addClass("active");
      }

    });

  })();
</script>

<script>
/*
  Adds some properties to the cart object so we can better differentiate products by vendor.
  */
  $(document).on("cart.ready cart.requestComplete", function(e, cart){

    var speaxItems = [];
    var thinxItems = [];
    var btwnItems  = [];
    var freeItems  = [];
    var hasDiscounts, hasCycleSetDiscount;

    $.each(cart.items, function(index, item){

      hasDiscounts        = false;
      hasCycleSetDiscount = false;

      // Loop thru discounts in item.
      $.each(item.discounts, function(index, discount){

        // Check for custom set discount.
        if(discount.title.indexOf("Custom Set") !== -1){
          hasCycleSetDiscount = true;
          return false;
        }

      });

      if(!hasCycleSetDiscount && item.discounts.length){
        hasDiscounts = true;
      }

      // Set has_non_cycleset_discount prop in item.
      item.has_non_cycleset_discount = hasDiscounts;

      if(item.vendor === "Speax"){
        speaxItems.push(item);
      }

      if(item.vendor === "THINX"){
        thinxItems.push(item);
      }

      if(item.vendor === "BTWN"){
        btwnItems.push(item);
      }

      // #gwp
      if(item.vendor === "Free Laundress gift set"){
        freeItems.push(item);
      }

      // Set line item index. Rivets will reference this for position of line items.
      item.line_index = index;

    });

    // Add to cart object.
    cart.speax_items = speaxItems;
    cart.thinx_items = thinxItems;
    cart.btwn_items  = btwnItems;
    cart.free_items  = freeItems;

    // Check for leaders discount in storage and apply discount to cart total.
    if(sessionStorage["leaders-program-name"]){
      cart.total_price    = cart.total_price - 1000;
      cart.total_discount = cart.total_discount + 1000;
    }

  });

  $(document).ready(function() {
    CartJS.init({"token":"26c66c82a1cf6a6d9063c88a61565f14","note":null,"attributes":{},"original_total_price":0,"total_price":0,"total_discount":0,"total_weight":0.0,"item_count":0,"items":[],"requires_shipping":false,"currency":"USD","items_subtotal_price":0,"cart_level_discount_applications":[]});
  });
</script>

      $caShipNotice:    $(".js-canada-ship-notice"),
      mustache_tags:    ['<%', '%>'],
      country:          ".shipping-dropdown__country",
      dropdownVisible:  "shipping-dropdown--visible"
    };

    var populateDropdownContent = function(){

      var markup = [];

      // Loop thru all zones to get their countries.
      $.each(zones, function(zoneIndex, zone){

        $.each(zone.countries, function(countryIndex, country){

          // Gather markup to insert into dropdown content.
          markup.push("<div class='shipping-dropdown__country' data-country='" + country + "'>" + country + "</div>");

        });

      });

      // Insert all content at once.
      s.$dropdownContent.html(markup);

    };

    /*
    @param {string} country - A country from the list of countries we ship to.
    @returns {object|undefined} returnedZone - Zone object if a zone was found or undefined if not.
    Checks if a given country exists in a shipping zone.
    */
    var getZoneForCountry = function(country){

      var returnedZone;

      $.each(zones, function(index, zone){

        if(zone.countries.indexOf(country) !== -1){
          returnedZone = zone;
          return false;
        }

      });

      return returnedZone;

    };

    var toggleIntShippingNotice = function(country){

      if(country === "United States" || country === "Canada"){
        s.$intShipNotice.slideUp(250);
      } else {
        s.$intShipNotice.slideDown(250);
      }

      if(country === "Canada"){
        s.$caShipNotice.slideDown(250);
      } else {
        s.$caShipNotice.slideUp(250);
      }

    };

    /*
    Displays speeds and rates markup on the front-end.
    */
    var showSpeedsAndRates = function(country){

      // Get zone.
      var zone = getZoneForCountry(country);

      // Set data for mustache.
      var mustacheOutput = Mustache.render(s.$speedsTemplate.html(), {
        country: country,
        zone: zone,
        speeds: zone.speeds
      });

      // Render mustache to the DOM.
      s.$speedsTarget.html(mustacheOutput);

    };

    /*
    Event listeners.
    */
    var events = function(){

      // Dropdown country element click.
      $(document).on("click", s.country, function(){

        // Get country name.
        var country = $(this).data("country");

        // Hide dropdown content.
        if( $("html").hasClass("no-touchevents") ){
          s.$dropdown.trigger("mouseleave");
        } else {
          s.$dropdown.removeClass(s.dropdownVisible);
        }

        // Update dropdown summary text.
        s.$dropdownSummary.text(country);

        // Display speeds and rates data to the user.
        showSpeedsAndRates(country);

        // Toggle international shipping notice.
        toggleIntShippingNotice(country);

      });

      if( $("html").hasClass("no-touchevents") ){

        // Dropdown mouseenter/leave.
        s.$dropdown.on("mouseenter", function(){
          // Reveal dropdown content.
          s.$dropdown.addClass(s.dropdownVisible);
        })
        .on("mouseleave", function(){

          // Hide dropdown content.
          s.$dropdown.removeClass(s.dropdownVisible);

        });

      } else {

        // Dropdown summary click.
        s.$dropdownSummary.on("click", function(){
          // Reveal dropdown content.
          s.$dropdown.toggleClass(s.dropdownVisible);
        });

      }

      $(document).on("doneWaitingForDY", function(e, data){

        var country;

        if(data.found){
          country = DY.geoFields.country;
        } else {
          country = "United States";
        }

        // Kick things off by auto selecting a country.
        $(s.country).filter("[data-country='" + country + "']")
        .trigger("click");

      });

    };

    /*
    Init all functions.
    */
    var init = function(){

      // Set mustache tags so it doesnt conflict with liquid.
			Mustache.tags = s.mustache_tags;

      // Listen for events.
      events();

      // Populate dropdown content with countries.
      populateDropdownContent();

    };

    init();

  })();
</script>
